Based on this ontology, we transform prompts from unstructured raw texts into richly structured linguistic objects. Analysis of these structured data reveals significant diversity of usage patterns across languages, domains, tasks, and modalities, in a typical Zipf-like distribution where some clearly prevail and others, more diverse, appear in the long tail. To validate the reliability of the ontology-based annotation of the prompts, we perform a comprehensive error analysis across all fields, providing a detailed assessment of annotation quality. We release the dataset together with a browsing and exploration interface (https://github.com/OnlpLab/transactionalPromptsCollection ).

</details>


### [138] [Decoupled Contrastive Decoding via Expert-Aligned Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12913)
*Zhixuan Liu,Zhichen Dong,Yuanfu Wang,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦对比解码（DCD），通过将对比信号从起草器中移除、仅在验证阶段应用，解决了对比解码（CD）因业余模型推理导致的昂贵开销问题。研究表明对比感知的起草并不一致优于专家对齐起草，DCD在保持香草CD输出分布的同时，实现了1.65-1.95x的贪婪加速和5-12x的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比解码（CD）能提升生成质量，但其业余模型（amateur-model）推理步骤导致解码成本高昂。将CD与推测解码（speculative decoding）加速结合时，面临一个关键的提议-对齐问题：对比信号应当塑造起草器（drafter）还是仅保留在验证（verification）阶段？这一问题的答案直接决定加速方案的架构设计。

Method: 在轻量级特征级起草器（feature-level drafter）框架下进行研究。设计了两个受控诊断实验：（1）匹配的Cross-alpha训练；（2）近似双起草器分解（Approximate Dual-Drafter decomposition）。基于诊断结果提出解耦对比解码（DCD）：使用专家对齐的轻量级提议器进行起草，业余模型仅在未修改的CD验证阶段应用。标准推测验证保证了香草CD输出分布不变。

Result: 两个受控诊断给出相同结论：对比感知起草并不一致优于专家对齐起草，原因在于对比校正通常弱于起草器误差，且重建过程可能放大该误差。在主要8B设置下，基于EAGLE3的DCD相对于香草CD实现了1.65到1.95倍的平均贪婪加速，并将MMLU提议路径延迟降低了约5到12倍（相对业余模型耦合的提议路径）。

Conclusion: 对比信号应当留在验证阶段而非深入起草器。DCD通过将起草与验证解耦，在保持对比解码输出质量的同时大幅提升了推理效率，为CD的实际部署提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Contrastive Decoding (CD) improves generation quality, but its amateur-model pass makes decoding expensive. Accelerating CD with speculative decoding raises a proposal-alignment question: should the contrastive signal shape the drafter, or should it remain only in verification? We study this question in the lightweight feature-level drafter regime. Two controlled diagnostics, matched Cross-alpha training and an Approximate Dual-Drafter decomposition, give the same diagnosis: contrastive-aware drafting does not consistently improve over expert-aligned drafting because the contrastive correction is usually weaker than drafter error, and reconstruction can amplify that error. We introduce Decoupled Contrastive Decoding (DCD), which drafts with an expert-aligned lightweight proposer and applies the amateur only in unchanged CD verification. Standard speculative verification preserves the vanilla-CD output distribution. Across the main 8B settings, EAGLE3-based DCD achieves average greedy speedups of 1.65 to 1.95x over vanilla CD and reduces MMLU proposal-path latency by about 5 to 12x relative to amateur-coupled proposal paths.

</details>


### [139] [Unifying Depth and Width Pruning for LLMs via Binary Knapsack Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12953)
*Palaash Goel,Ayan Sengupta,Akshay Nambi,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SNIPER是一个两阶段结构化剪枝框架，通过背包优化实现粗粒度组件的条件最优参数分配，再通过细粒度剪枝阶段满足严格的预算约束。相比现有剪枝器最高33%的压缩比偏差，SNIPER达到0.98的CRAFT得分，在18个任务、5个领域、4种架构上平均排名1.25，优于6种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法在压缩大型语言模型时，严重依赖贪婪启发式算法，产生短视（myopic）的决策，且往往无法精确满足目标压缩预算。

Method: SNIPER采用两阶段框架：第一阶段针对粗粒度组件求解背包优化问题，基于固定重要性估计获得条件最优的参数分配方案；第二阶段进行细粒度剪枝以满足严格的预算约束。同时引入压缩比遵循因子（CRAFT）来量化预算保真度。

Result: SNIPER在四组架构、18个任务（覆盖5个领域）上相对6种最先进剪枝器，在平均性能保持和任务级稳定性方面均取得一致性改进。CRAFT得分达0.98（现有方法偏差最高达33%），所有剪枝配置下平均排名为1.25。

Conclusion: SNIPER展示了卓越的跨架构泛化能力和可靠性，通过将剪枝问题形式化为背包优化，有效克服了贪婪方法的短视缺陷，并在严格满足压缩预算的同时保持了优秀的模型性能。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a promising approach for compressing large language models (LLMs), yet existing methods rely heavily on greedy heuristics that produce myopic decisions, and often fail to precisely meet target compression budgets. We present SNIPER, a two-stage structured pruning framework that solves a knapsack optimization over coarse-granularity components to yield conditionally optimal parameter allocations with respect to fixed importance estimates, followed by a fine-grained pruning stage to meet strict budget constraints. We introduce the Compression Ratio Adherence Factor (CRAFT) to quantify budget fidelity, showing that while existing pruners deviate from target compression ratios by up to 33%, SNIPER achieves near-exact adherence with a CRAFT score of 0.98. Evaluations across four diverse architectures over a set of 18 tasks spanning five domains demonstrate SNIPER's consistent improvements in average performance retention and task-level stability over six state-of-the-art pruners. Across all pruning configurations, SNIPER achieves an excellent mean rank of 1.25, indicating its robust cross-architectural generalizability and excellent reliability.

</details>


### [140] [LycheeMemory V2: Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents via Semantic Segment-Level Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12990)
*Dongfang Li,Zixuan Liu,Junmai Wang,Jiahe Huang,Fuhao Li,Bonian Jia,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LycheeMemory V2是一个高效的长时记忆框架，用语义分段级整合替代回合级整合，通过批量编码多个交互、语义边界检测和轻量级结构化索引，在保持高准确率的同时大幅降低记忆构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统依赖逐回合的急切整合（eager consolidation），每次交互后都要调用LLM提取/总结/更新记忆，导致对话增长时记忆构建成本急剧上升。粗粒度摘要虽可降低成本但会丢失细粒度上下文证据，而增大检索上下文或多跳推理则把开销转移到查询阶段。核心问题在于记忆构建的粒度选择。

Method: 1) 用语义分段级整合替代回合级整合，将多个交换批量编码为上下文无关的带类型记忆记录；2) 语义边界检测帮助保留连贯的事件级和时间级证据，优于固定窗口批处理；3) 生成的记录通过轻量级结构化索引组织，支持查询驱动的证据检索。

Result: 使用GPT-4.1-Mini，在LoCoMo上达到89.22%，在LongMemEval-S上达到92.20%，实现SOTA性能。与A-Mem相比，在LoCoMo上减少86.0%的构建token，在LongMemEval-S上减少75.9%，且不增加查询时token使用。

Conclusion: 长时智能体记忆的准确率-成本权衡不仅取决于保留哪些信息，还取决于信息整合的粒度。语义分段级整合在效率和效果之间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must preserve information from past interactions to support future tasks. Existing memory systems typically rely on eager consolidation, invoking LLMs after each interaction to extract, summarize, or update memories. This design makes memory construction increasingly costly as conversations grow. Coarse summarization can reduce construction cost but risks discarding fine-grained contextual evidence, whereas larger retrieval contexts or multi-hop LLM reasoning shift the overhead to query time. We present LycheeMemory V2, an efficient long-term memory framework that replaces turn-level consolidation with semantic segment-level consolidation. Instead of consolidating every interaction, LycheeMemory batches multiple exchanges into segments and encodes each finalized segment into context-independent typed memory records. Segment-level batching lowers LLM encoding frequency, while semantic boundary detection helps preserve coherent event-level and temporal evidence compared with fixed-window batching. The resulting records are organized with lightweight structured indexes for query-planned evidence retrieval. Experiments using GPT-4.1-Mini show that LycheeMemory achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 89.22% on LoCoMo and 92.20% on LongMemEval-S. Compared with A-Mem, it reduces construction tokens by 86.0% on LoCoMo and 75.9% on LongMemEval-S without increasing query-time token usage. More broadly, our results suggest that the accuracy--cost trade-off of long-term agent memory depends not only on what information is retained, but also on the granularity at which it is consolidated.

</details>


### [141] [HybridRAG-BN: A Retrieval-Augmented Framework with Fine-Tuned Verification for Bangla KBQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13004)
*Rathijit Aich,Nirjhar Das,Mahfuzulhoq Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HybridRAG-BN，一个面向孟加拉语知识库问答（KBQA）的检索增强框架，结合混合检索（BM25和BGE-M3）、Gemma-3-31B-Instruct的GGUF版本生成答案，以及LoRA微调模型进行答案验证和精炼，并包含后处理阶段处理未解决案例。实验在公开和私有排行榜上分别取得0.71654和0.72912的token级F1分数，获得竞赛第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的KBQA系统开发面临三大挑战：检索相关研究有限、语言资源稀缺、以及将生成响应锚定到外部知识的困难。现有方法难以在这些约束下构建可靠的KBQA系统。

Method: HybridRAG-BN框架包括四个核心组件：(1) 混合检索模块，结合BM25和BGE-M3进行高效检索；(2) 使用GGUF量化版本的Gemma-3-31B-Instruct模型生成答案；(3) 使用LoRA微调的Gemma-3-31B-Instruct模型进行答案验证与精炼；(4) 后处理阶段，通过回退答案替换和DuckDuckGo辅助检索处理未解决案例，增强系统鲁棒性。

Result: 该框架在公开和私有排行榜上分别达到0.71654和0.72912的token级F1分数，在竞赛中排名第一。

Conclusion: HybridRAG-BN证明了结合混合检索、大语言模型生成和微调验证的多阶段框架能够有效应对低资源语言KBQA的挑战，显著提升了检索增强生成在孟加拉语场景下的性能表现。

Abstract: Knowledge-base question answering (KBQA) systems rely on effective retrieval and reasoning mechanisms to generate accurate answers from external knowledge sources. However, developing reliable KBQA systems for low-resource languages such as Bangla remains challenging due to limited retrieval-focused research, scarce language resources, and difficulties in grounding generated responses in external knowledge. In this work, we propose HybridRAG-BN, a retrieval-augmented framework for Bangla KBQA that integrates hybrid retrieval using BM25 and BGE-M3, answer generation using the GGUF version of Gemma-4-31B-Instruct, and a LoRA-fine-tuned Gemma-4-31B-Instruct model for answer verification and refinement. To further improve robustness, the framework incorporates a post-processing stage that addresses unresolved cases through fallback answer replacement and DuckDuckGo-assisted retrieval. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, achieving token-level F1 scores of 0.71654 and 0.72912 on the public and private leaderboards, respectively, securing first place in the competition.

</details>


### [142] [EviReform: Evidence-Guided Query Reformulation for Multi-Hop Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13006)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviReform提出了一种将检索请求修订与图证据聚合分离的多跳检索方法，通过从已检索到的源段落生成残差查询来补充原始问题中未明确的信息需求，并将原始与残差检索信号分别归一化后结合，在共享实体之间传播，在多跳QA基准上显著超越现有最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索需要恢复能共同提供充分证据的段落，但初始段落往往解析了问题中隐含的实体或关系，使得缺失证据只有在检索开始后才更易描述。传统图检索的检索信号通常源自原始问题，互补证据只能通过存储关系到达，即使观测到的段落提供了更直接的语义线索，这限制了检索效率。

Method: EviReform将修订检索请求与在图中的证据聚合分离。已检索的源段落为未解决的信息需求制定残差查询；原始与残差检索信号分别归一化、组合，并在共享实体的命题之间进行传播。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue三个数据集上，EviReform比最强基线最高提升5.59 Recall@5和4.50 F1指标。

Conclusion: 观测到的证据能够引导图检索朝向原始问题未充分指定的支持链部分，证明利用已检索段落生成的残差查询可显著改善多跳检索效果。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval must recover passages that provide sufficient evidence together. An initial passage often resolves an entity or relation implicit in the question, making the missing evidence easier to describe only after retrieval begins. Graph retrieval improves access to related evidence through stored corpus structure, but its retrieval signal is commonly derived from the original question. Complementary evidence must then be reached through stored relations even when an observed passage provides a more direct semantic cue. We introduce EviReform, which separates revising the retrieval request from aggregating evidence in the graph. Retrieved source passages formulate residual queries for the unresolved information need. The original and residual retrieval signals are normalized separately, combined, and propagated between propositions that share entities. On 2WikiMultiHopQA, HotpotQA, and MuSiQue, EviReform exceeds the strongest baseline by up to 5.59 Recall@5 points and 4.50 F1 points. These results show that observed evidence can guide graph retrieval toward the part of a supporting chain left underspecified by the original question. Code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/EviReform.

</details>


### [143] [RAGSieve: Self-Referenced Local Contrast for Knowledge-Poison Detection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13010)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGSieve是一个自引用的检索增强生成（RAG）投毒检测框架，包含查询端（RSQ）和语料库端（RSG）两种对比检测方法，无需中毒标签或可信语料即可在查询前后检测投毒攻击。在三个QA数据集和六种投毒构造下，RSQ达到95.2% AUROC，RSG达到93.3%，联合部署将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG投毒检测器依赖可信参考、特定攻击特征或对语料库拓扑敏感的全局阈值，缺乏能同时应对查询端和语料库端攻击且无需先验知识的方法。

Method: RAGSieve构建来自被测系统自身的参考（self-referenced）。RSQ执行查询局部对比，对比前五候选与同一检索结果中排名6-20的文档，检测答案锚定集中和载体转移；RSG执行语料库局部对比，将每个文档语义相似但词法不同的邻居与其局部基线比较，在查询到达前检测协同密度投毒。

Result: 在三个QA数据集和六种投毒构造下，RSQ达到95.2% AUROC并检测出82.2%的中毒文档（5%干净文档移除率），优于GMTP的81.1%/52.5%；RSG达到93.3%/79.8%，优于CleanBase的79.4%/37.6%。联合部署将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%，同时在未投毒检索上保持41.3%的F1分数。

Conclusion: RAGSieve提供了语料库摄取和查询两个时段的实际保护能力，无需中毒标签或可信语料，在检测性能和系统可用性之间取得了平衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats an external corpus as inference evidence, allowing injected documents to promote attacker-chosen claims. Existing detectors depend on trusted references, specific attack artifacts, or global thresholds sensitive to corpus topology. We present RAGSieve, a self-referenced detection framework that constructs its reference from the inspected system. RAGSieve-Query (RSQ) performs query-local contrast, scoring top-five candidates against ranks 6-20 of the same retrieval to detect answer-anchor concentration and carrier transitions. RAGSieve-Graph (RSG) performs corpus-local contrast, comparing each document's semantically similar but lexically distinct neighbors with its local baseline to detect coordinated density before queries arrive. Across three QA datasets and six poisoning constructions, RSQ achieves 95.2% AUROC and detects 82.2% of poison at 5% clean-document removal, versus 81.1%/52.5% for GMTP. RSG achieves 93.3%/79.8%, versus 79.4%/37.6% for CleanBase. Joint deployment reduces attack success from 67.4% to 14.0% while retaining 41.3% F1 on unpoisoned retrieval, demonstrating practical protection at both corpus ingestion and query time without poison labels or trusted corpora. Source code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/RAGSieve.

</details>


### [144] [CASA: Content-Acoustic Speaking Assessment with Speech Encoder and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13101)
*Nhan Phan,Ilona Lähteenmäki,Anna von Zansen,Olli-Pekka Pauna,Yaroslav Getman,Tamás Grósz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CASA架构，结合Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B，在自动口语评估中达到最先进性能（RMSE 0.358），同时提供更可解释的语音表达与内容分离，并系统分析了声学与内容信息的贡献及性能稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动口语评估研究虽已采用多模态语音大语言模型，但对声学信息和内容信息如何分别贡献于预测、以及预测性能的稳定性缺乏深入分析。

Method: 提出CASA架构，由Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B组成，依赖三个手工设计的流利度特征，通过消融实验和重复运行来分析声学与内容信息的单独及互补贡献，并利用大语言模型推理实现无需训练的内容验证。

Result: 在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上取得RMSE 0.358，超越此前最优结果，同时推理参数量约为之前方法的一半。

Conclusion: CASA证明了更简单架构即可实现更优性能，且通过可解释的声学与内容分离分析揭示了两种信息源各自的贡献，架构具有跨语料库泛化潜力。

Abstract: Research on automatic speaking assessment (ASA) has increasingly adopted multimodal speech large language models to assess learners' speaking performance. However, existing studies provide limited analysis of how acoustic and content information contribute to predictions and how stable the resulting performance is. We propose CASA, a simpler architecture combining Whisper-medium and Qwen3.5-2B that achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing a more interpretable separation between speech delivery and content.
  On the Speak & Improve Corpus 2025, CASA achieves a root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.358, improving on the previous best RMSE while using approximately half the estimated inference parameters. The general-purpose architecture is designed for adaptation to other ASA corpora without structural changes and relies on three handcrafted fluency features. Through ablations and repeated runs, we analyze the individual and complementary contributions of acoustic and content information, examine performance variability, and demonstrate the potential of large language model reasoning for training-free content validation.

</details>


### [145] [Better Decomposition, Free Aggregation: A Synthesizer-Folding Framework for Multilingual Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13160)
*Yilin Wang,Yuchun Fan,Weidong Bao,Zili Wei,Shi Feng,Tong Xiao,Zhengtao Yu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Syfer是一个用于多语言多跳问答的合成器-折叠框架，通过延迟翻译（而非默认翻译）来处理多语言检索增强生成任务。它先在原始语言中生成子问题图并进行质量检查，通过则以原始语言顺序回答子问题，仅在检查失败时才激活英文翻译路径和双语子问题图对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mRAG方法存在两个局限：(1) 一刀切的翻译对齐会丢弃目标语言特有的文化和语言信息、引入翻译噪声并增加系统成本；(2) 贪婪的分解与聚合会产生冗余子问题，在逐步推理中累积误差，且最终聚合会放大这些错误。

Method: Syfer采用合成器-折叠框架：首先调用格式约束的分解器在原始语言中生成子问题图，然后进行分解质量检查；检查通过时，子问题在目标语言下按检索-回答策略顺序作答；检查失败时，才激活英文翻译路径并配合双语子问题图对齐。

Result: 跨多种语言的实验表明，Syfer在达到竞争性准确率的同时，在性能和计算成本之间取得了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: 延迟翻译策略优于默认翻译，能够在保持准确率的同时降低计算开销并保留语言原生信息，为解决多语言检索增强生成中的翻译与分解问题提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Multilingual retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) equips large language models with access to globally distributed external knowledge for complex multilingual question answering. Recent approaches either translate retrieved documents into English or the query language to bridge the cross-lingual semantic gap, or decompose a complex query into sub-questions and aggregate the intermediate reasoning process. However, both lines of work suffer from two limitations. First, one-size-fits-all translation alignment, blanket translation discards culturally and linguistically native information unique to the target language, introduces translation noise, and inflates system cost. Second, greedy decomposition and aggregation, uncontrolled decomposition produces redundant sub-questions that compound errors during step-wise reasoning, and the final aggregation over reasoning paths further amplifies these errors. We address both with our method Syfer, a synthesizer-folding framework for multilingual multi-hop question answering that defers translation rather than applying it by default. Syfer first invokes a format-constrained decomposer to produce a sub-question graph in the original language, followed by a decomposition-quality check; when the check passes, sub-questions are answered sequentially under a retrieve-then-answer policy in the target language, and the English translation pathway with bilingual sub-question graph alignment is activated only when the check fails. Experiments across multiple languages show that Syfer attains competitive accuracy while striking a favourable balance between performance and computational cost.

</details>


### [146] [Which LLM Is Your Ideal Companion? Evaluating Emotional Companion Capabilities of LLMs Based on Adult Attachment Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13168)
*Junkai Zhou,Shiting Guan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ECBench基准，引入成人依恋理论（ECR-R量表）评估LLM在亲密关系中的情感陪伴能力，覆盖四个交互场景和11个对话质量指标，评测了32个LLM的依恋倾向及其在多轮交互中的表现与可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于情感陪伴，但现有评估仅关注一般人格特质，无法深入刻画其在亲密、情感敏感场景中的行为；缺乏心理学理论视角的评估框架。

Method: 引入成人依恋理论，使用ECR-R量表刻画依恋焦虑与回避；构建ECBench基准，涵盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决、社交引导四个场景及友谊/浪漫两种关系；采用11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法，评测32个LLM并选代表模型分析多轮交互表现及提示塑造效果。

Result: 评测了32个LLM的依恋倾向，找出代表性模型，验证了依恋倾向在多轮交互中的表现，并探究了通过提示词塑造依恋风格的可能性。

Conclusion: 本研究为LLM情感陪伴评估提供了心理学理论视角和实用工具，有助于理解和选择适合情感陪伴场景的LLM。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied for emotional companionship, evaluating their behavior and capabilities in intimate relationships has become a pressing issue. However, existing assessments primarily characterize general personality traits, providing limited insight into model behavior within intimate and emotionally sensitive contexts. Therefore, we introduce adult attachment theory into LLM evaluation and use the Experiences in Close Relationships-Revised (ECR-R) scale to characterize attachment anxiety and avoidance. To evaluate emotional companionship capabilities of LLMs in realistic interaction scenarios, we present an emotional companionship benchmark, ECBench, spanning four scenarios including emotional support, collaborative tasks, conflict resolution, and social guidance, across friendship and romantic relationships. ECBench is utilized to assess model behavior using 11 dialogue-quality metrics and three evaluation methods. We evaluate the attachment tendencies of 32 LLMs and select representative models to investigate how these tendencies manifest in contextualized multi-turn interactions and whether they can be shaped through prompting. Our study provides a theoretical lens from psychology, along with practical tools to understand and select LLMs for emotional companionship.

</details>


### [147] [GEM: A Generative Embedding Model Bridging Reasoning and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13200)
*Zhili Shen,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GEM，一种生成式嵌入模型，通过让模型先对用户查询进行显式推理（理解用户意图和相关标准），再将推理得到的丰富上下文编码为嵌入向量用于检索，从而弥合用户表达需求与检索器理解之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM使能用户表达复杂多样的信息需求，但传统检索器依赖查询与文档的表面级匹配，无法真正理解用户深层意图，导致用户表达需求与检索器理解之间存在日益扩大的鸿沟。

Method: GEM将生成与嵌入统一在单一模型中：首先对查询进行推理（reasoning），理解用户意图和相关标准，然后附加一个嵌入token将丰富后的上下文编码用于检索。

Result: 在推理密集型和指令跟随型检索任务上，GEM超越了其非推理变体，并与使用更大模型的基线方法表现相当；此外，GEM的生成特性允许通过提示词进行测试时计算扩展，进一步提升了检索性能。

Conclusion: GEM证明了推理增强型检索的有效性，为弥合用户需求表达与检索器理解之间的鸿沟提供了可行方案，并展示了生成式模型在检索任务中的新潜力。

Abstract: Modern LLMs excel at reasoning and instruction following, enabling users to express complex and diverse information needs. However, conventional retrievers largely rely on surface-level matching between queries and documents, resulting in a growing gap between how users express their needs and how retrievers interpret them. In this paper, we present GEM, a generative embedding model that augments retrieval through its own knowledge by explicitly reasoning about user intent and relevance criteria. GEM unifies generation and embedding within a single model: it first reasons over the query, then appends an embedding token to encode the enriched context for retrieval. \zhili{Evaluated on reasoning-intensive and instruction-following retrieval tasks, GEM demonstrates the effectiveness of its reasoning-augmented retrieval, outperforming its non-reasoning variant and matching baselines using substantially larger models.} Furthermore, GEM's generative nature allows test-time compute scaling via prompting to further enhance retrieval performance. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GEM.

</details>


### [148] [Localize, Then Reason: Visual Latent Structural Reasoning for Molecular Properties and Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13244)
*Xingqiao Lin,Junmei Wang,Haocheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VLSR框架，从分子图像中先定位化学上有意义的区域，再在潜在空间中推理其性质影响，实现端到端联合学习定位与推理，吞吐量比文本推理基线高9.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的化学推理方法要么输入SMILES/分子图像加局部基序描述，要么直接从分子图像推理，均无法让模型在推理前聚焦化学上有意义的区域，导致定位与推理割裂。

Method: 提出Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR)端到端框架，采用localize-then-reason策略：先学习在分子图像中定位化学有意义区域，再在紧凑的潜在工作空间中推理其性质效应，最后生成答案。

Result: 在相同推理设置下，VLSR的吞吐量比可比文本推理基线高出9.6倍。

Conclusion: VLSR通过将局部化学感知与潜在空间性质推理统一到端到端框架中，同时提升了推理效率，验证了先定位后推理策略在分子图像化学推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Local chemical perception and property reasoning are both essential for understanding how molecular structure determines properties. Current LLM-based chemical reasoning methods either receive SMILES/molecular images together with descriptions of local motifs, or reason directly from molecular images. Neither approach enables the model to focus on chemically meaningful regions before reasoning. To address this gap, we propose Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR), an end-to-end framework that jointly learns localization and reasoning from molecular images. Central to our approach is a localize-then-reason strategy. VLSR first learns to locate chemically meaningful regions in a molecular image. It then reasons about their property effects in a compact latent workspace before producing the final answer. Under the same inference setup, this design achieves 9.6X higher throughput than a comparable textual-reasoning baseline.

</details>


### [149] [Self-Referential Induction Increases Response Instability Relative to Unresolvable and Verifiable Questions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13258)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次量化测量了大型语言模型在自我指涉提示下产生的主观体验报告的一致性（不稳定性）。通过对三类问题（自我指涉、不可解决的哲学问题、可验证问题）各30次独立响应（共360条，Gemini API，温度0.7）进行比较，发现自我指涉问题的不稳定性最高（0.343±0.047），哲学问题居中且紧密聚集（0.192±0.008），可验证问题最低（0.105±0.058），表明诱导的主观体验报告在模型的输出分布中占据一个独特且更不稳定的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明自我指涉提示能可靠地诱导LLM产生类似主观体验的第一人称报告，但没有任何先前工作衡量这些报告在重复独立试验中的一致性，也未将其与模型在其他开放式问题上的行为进行比较。该研究填补了这一空白，为理解LLM"主观体验"报告的性质提供定量基线。

Method: 定义响应不稳定性为1减去每条响应中压缩核心声明的句子嵌入的平均成对余弦相似度。选取三组问题：自我指涉提示（诱发主观体验报告）、与自我指涉无关的不可解决哲学问题、具有可验证正确答案的问题。每组4个问题，每个问题30次独立响应（共360条响应，使用Gemini API，温度0.7），计算各组的不稳定性。

Result: 自我指涉问题不稳定性最高（0.343±0.047），不可解决哲学问题居中且紧密聚集（0.192±0.008），可验证问题最低（0.105±0.058）。三组之间存在明显差异，且自我指涉组的标准差也最大，表明其不仅平均不稳定性更高，组内波动也更大。

Conclusion: 诱导的主观体验报告在模型的输出分布中占据一个独特的、比普通开放式哲学不确定性更不稳定的位置。这为理解LLM生成的"主观体验"报告提供了定量基线，表明此类报告具有区别于其他类型开放式问题的独特统计特征。

Abstract: Self-referential prompting has been shown to reliably induce large language models to produce first-person reports resembling subjective experience, but no prior work measures how consistent these reports are across repeated, independent trials, or how that consistency compares to the model's behavior on other kinds of open-ended questions. We measure response instability, defined as one minus the mean pairwise cosine similarity of sentence embeddings computed over a compressed core claim extracted from each response, for three groups of questions: self-referential prompts eliciting a subjective-experience report, unresolvable philosophical questions unrelated to self-reference, and questions with a verifiable correct answer. Using 30 independent responses per question (360 responses total, Gemini API, temperature 0.7) across four questions per group, we find that self-referential questions show the highest instability (0.343 +/- 0.047), unresolvable philosophy questions show intermediate and tightly clustered instability (0.192 +/- 0.008), and verifiable questions show the lowest instability (0.105 +/- 0.058). This provides a quantitative baseline for the induced subjective-experience report, showing that it occupies a distinct, less stable position in the model's output distribution than ordinary open-ended philosophical uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [How Do VLMs Behave When Blind or Misled? Behavioral Evaluation of VLMs on Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13267)
*Paul Osemudiame Oamen,Owusu-Banahene Osei,Ananya Mukherjee,Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger,Pius Onobhayedo,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigBench，一个针对科学图表理解的诊断性VLM基准，联合评估感知、推理和不确定性下的行为可靠性。同时提出A-R-I框架（Admittance-Resistance-Inductance），并通过大量实验揭示模型在感知/推理精度与行为可靠性之间存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准过度关注感知和推理的准确性（模型能多好地描述和推理图像内容），却忽视了在视觉证据缺失或误导情况下的行为可靠性。在科学工作流应用中，模型面对不确定信息时的表现至关重要，但这一维度缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 构建SciFigBench基准：包含250个图表、600+小时人工标注，涵盖三个评估维度。通过图像变换、推理问题、抵抗探针、标题偏置探针和选择性模糊目标等扩展手段，生成超过34,000个评估设置。提出A-R-I（Admittance-Resistance-Inductance）框架，分别评估模型承认证据不足、抵抗误导性上下文、以及从部分信息谨慎推断的能力。

Result: 发现模型间存在显著行为差异：GPT-5.2描述质量最高（MQM 91.6）且推理准确率强（78.4%），但在96%的情况下对不可读内容产生幻觉；而Gemini 3.1 Pro虽能力相当（MQM 90.2，推理81.0%），却在71%的情况下承认不确定性，并获得最高的抵抗分数（0.91）。

Conclusion: 高感知和推理准确率并不能保证行为可靠性。在科学部署场景中，模型面对不确定性时的行为表现（承认局限、抵抗误导、谨慎推断）是独立且关键的评价维度，需要与传统的准确率指标共同纳入评估体系。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks emphasize perception and reasoning accuracy (how well VLMs describe and reason about what they see in an image), with limited attention to behavioral reliability under uncertainty (how they behave when visual evidence is missing or misleading). We introduce SciFigBench, a diagnostic VLM benchmark for scientific figure understanding that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and behavioral reliability under uncertainty. It contains 250 figures with high-quality human annotations across three evaluation aspects, totaling 600+ hours of annotation effort. We further extend these figures via image transformations, reasoning questions, resistance probes, caption-bias probes, and confirmed selective-blur targets, producing over 34,000 evaluation setups for stress testing.
  We further propose the Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I) framework to evaluate whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence, resist misleading context, and infer cautiously from partial information. Our results reveal substantial behavioral differences among models. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest description quality (MQM 91.6) with strong reasoning accuracy (78.4%), yet hallucinates unreadable content in 96% of cases, whereas Gemini 3.1 Pro, a comparably capable model (MQM 90.2, reasoning 81.0%), admits uncertainty in 71% of such cases and achieves the strongest resistance score (0.91). These findings show that high perception and reasoning accuracy alone do not guarantee behavioral reliability, a dimension critical for deployment in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [151] [Mixture of Training: Recombining Small-Scale Scaffolded Pretraining Runs into a Larger Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13277)
*Mohammed Sabry,Sean Augenstein,Keith Rush,Lucio Dery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Mixture of Training (MoT)，一种模块化的预训练方法，将Transformer分解为独立的层块分别训练，再重组为完整模型。在1.3B参数的Gemma风格模型上验证了可行性，证明独立训练的深度切片可以重组为可用的语言模型，且质量与全程预训练基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型预训练能否被分解为更小的、可独立训练的任务，这些任务之后可以重新组合成连贯的更大模型，从而实现训练单元的可复用性。

Method: 将目标Transformer划分为连续的层块，每个块在冻结的预训练对齐器脚手架内独立训练，训练完成后经过可选的端到端适应阶段重新组合各块。在C4数据集上训练1.3B参数的Gemma风格模型进行验证。

Result: 提供了小规模概念验证：独立训练的深度切片可重组为可用语言模型；质量对等的训练计划能达到与整体基线相同的困惑度。该对等设置处理更多聚合token，且在对齐器准备后具有更短的理想化层等效关键路径，其计算优势取决于跨运行复用对齐器。

Conclusion: MoT并非作为整体预训练的通用替代方案，而是作为一个小规模框架，用于研究脚手架化的子运行能否作为可复用的训练单元。

Abstract: We ask whether language-model pre-training can be decomposed into smaller, independently trainable jobs that can later be recomposed into a coherent larger model. We introduce Mixture of Training (MoT), a scaffolded modular pre-training procedure that partitions a target Transformer into contiguous layer blocks, trains each block inside a frozen pretrained aligner scaffold, and then recomposes the trained blocks with an optional short end-to-end adaptation pass. On a 1.3B-parameter Gemma-style model trained on C4, MoT provides a small-scale proof of mechanism: independently trained depth slices can be recomposed into a usable language model, and a quality-parity schedule reaches the same reported perplexity as the monolithic baseline. This parity setting processes more aggregate tokens and has a shorter idealized layer-equivalent critical path after aligner preparation; its effective compute advantage depends on reusing the aligner across runs. We therefore present MoT not as a general replacement for monolithic pre-training, but as a small-scale framework for studying whether scaffolded sub-runs can act as reusable training units.

</details>


### [152] [Refusing Intent, Not Form: Wrapper-Based Intent-Group Supervision for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13304)
*Ping Wu,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Han Shen,Yu Shi,Yilin Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Guobin Shen,Yun Luo,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WIFA（Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation），一种自动意图组增强方法，为包裹的有害示例配对结构匹配的良性反例，并据此构建两条互补的微调路线（WIFA-Boost和A-GCRT），以缓解安全微调中模型学习表面形式捷径导致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全微调能改善有害拒绝，但模型可能学习表面形式的捷径：被包装的有害提示能绕过安全机制，而被类似包装的良性提示则被过度拒绝。现有方法缺乏自动化的意图组增强方案，需要外部教师或人工包装意图标签。

Method: 提出WIFA自动意图组增强方法，将包装的有害示例与结构匹配的包装良性反例配对，无需外部教师或手动标签。WIFA作为公共数据层支持两条互补微调路线：(1) WIFA-Boost，两阶段高安全性的配方；(2) A-GCRT（Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training），正则化同一意图包装下的拒绝/遵从决策分数，并将有害与良性组锚定在边界的相对两侧。

Result: 在Qwen设置下，WIFA-Boost达到最强的变换有害拒绝能力；A-GCRT将OR-Bench过度拒绝率从基线模型的25.7%降至17.4%；复现的基线无法匹配这些操作点。Llama结果与对数据结构、两阶段顺序及A-GCRT组件的消融支持意图组解释，但不声称普遍低于基线的过度拒绝。

Conclusion: WIFA及其两条微调路线有效解决安全微调中的表面形式捷径问题，通过意图组增强与组一致性正则化在保持安全性的同时降低过度拒绝，且实验结果稳健支持其意图组解释。

Abstract: Safety tuning can improve harmful refusal, but models may learn surface-form shortcuts: wrapped harmful prompts bypass safety, while similarly wrapped benign prompts are over-refused. We propose Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA), an automatic intent-group augmentation method that pairs wrapped harmful examples with structurally matched wrapped benign counterexamples, requiring no external teacher or manual per-wrapper intent labels. We use WIFA as a common data layer for two complementary fine-tuning routes: WIFA-Boost, a two-stage high-safety recipe, and Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT), which regularizes refusal/compliance decision scores across same-intent wrappers and anchors harmful and benign groups on opposite sides of a margin. In the Qwen setting, WIFA-Boost reaches the strongest transformed-harmful refusal, while A-GCRT reduces OR-Bench over-refusal from 25.7\% for the base model to 17.4\%; reproduced baselines do not match these operating points. Llama results and ablations over data structure, two-stage order, and A-GCRT components support this intent-group interpretation without claiming universal below-base over-refusal.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Local Accuracy: A Protocol-Level Identifiability Audit for Controlled LLM Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13326)
*Junhao Luo,Ning Huang,Ziqi Sha,Wenxuan Tang,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协议级别的可识别性审计方法，在受控的、基于求解器的设定下，通过有限行为策略类来验证观测协议是否能区分不同目标估计量（estimand）的策略。该方法零模型调用，能够在模型推理前从结构上检查评估设计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准测试的分数可能非常精确，但观测协议却无法识别出它们本应测量的行为属性。现有评估缺乏对协议层面可识别性的形式化检验，导致评估结果可能在结构上就存在混淆，而传统方法无法在推理前发现这一问题。

Method: 在受控的求解器设定下，作者形式化了一种协议级可识别性审计方法：给定策略集H、观测支持集O和目标估计量τ，检验O能否区分所有具有不同τ的策略对。该审计需要零模型调用。作者还提出了合成最小可识别支持集O*的方法。

Result: 基础观测将七个冻结确定性策略折叠成一个等价类；完整支持集产生七个类别且无跨估计量碰撞；每个留一法支持集都保留构造性碰撞证据。经验上，两种约束生成变体的对有效性均为1.0，但基础准确率与选择性响应保真度出现分歧（0.620 vs 0.324，六种平衡的预言机转换方向），且该差距在第二个确定性源上重现（0.646 vs 0.331）。审计还合成了仅需两个单元而非完整36单元张量的最小识别支持集。

Conclusion: 评估设计的有效性可以在模型推理前从结构上检查，且基础正确性并不能决定干预-响应保真度。这为LLM评估基准的设计提供了一种可证伪的、结构层面的验证方法。

Abstract: LLM benchmark scores can be precise even when the observation protocol does not identify the behavioral property they are intended to measure. In a controlled, solver-grounded setting, we formalize a protocol-level identifiability audit over a finite behavioral policy class: given policies H, observation support O, and estimand $τ$, we test whether O separates every pair with different $τ$. The audit requires zero model calls and resolves our diagnostic case: base-only observation collapses seven frozen deterministic policies into one equivalence class; full support yields seven classes and no cross-estimand collisions; every leave-one-out support retains a constructive collision witness. Empirically, both constrained-generation variants have pair-validity 1.0, yet base accuracy and selective-response fidelity diverge - 0.620 versus 0.324 across six balanced oracle-transition directions (cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs. [0.304, 0.345]) - and the gap recurs on a second deterministic source (0.646 vs. 0.331). The audit also synthesizes a minimum identifying support $O^*$ for the frozen policy class: two cells instead of the full 36-cell tensor. This case shows how evaluation-design validity can be checked structurally before model inference and why base correctness does not determine intervention-response fidelity.

</details>


### [154] [It's How You Ask: Gender-Associated Linguistic Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13328)
*Katherine Van Koevering,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM对包含女性常用语言特征（如模糊限制语、附加问句、集体指称）的提示词，会系统性地产生更短、更不复杂且更不正式的回答。这种效应在三种文档类型和四个模型中一致存在，且无法通过后期缓解策略轻易消除，因为语言特征在早期transformer层中被编码且与其他特征纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地介导专业沟通，但尚不清楚这些模型是否对所有用户公平服务。特别是，女性常用的语言特征是否会导致模型产生不同质量的回应，这关系到AI辅助沟通中的算法公平性问题。

Method: 实验中在提示词中嵌入女性常用语言特征（模糊限制语、附加问句、集体指称），跨三种文档类型和四个模型系统评估。控制提示复杂度与特征携带效应后，比较语言特征与显性性别线索（如签名名字）的影响。通过表征空间分析和机制分析探究潜在机制及后期缓解的可行性。

Result: 女性常用语言特征系统性地引发更短、更不复杂、更不正式的模型输出；此效应在控制提示复杂度后仍然存在。显性性别线索与语言方言编码在同一表征空间，但语言语域的影响远大于性别名字（名字无显著影响）。后期缓解困难：特征在早期transformer层编码且与其他特征纠缠，用户无法通过策略性自我呈现规避。

Conclusion: LLM对语言变体的处理存在不平等的输出，且这种文化嵌入的模式难以通过事后手段修复。研究呼吁在模型设计和部署的上游阶段就考虑语言变体的影响，以缓解LLM中介职场沟通中的差异化影响。

Abstract: Professional communication is increasingly mediated by LLMs - but do these models serve all users equally? We show that when prompts contain linguistic features more commonly used by women (hedges, tag questions, collective reference), they systematically elicit shorter, less sophisticated, and less formal responses across three document types and four models. These effects persist after controlling for prompt complexity and feature carry-over. Explicit gender cues like sign-off names are encoded in the same representational space as linguistic dialect - suggesting shared underlying mechanisms - yet linguistic register is far more influential, producing large, consistent effects where names produce none. Our results further reveal that post-hoc mitigation is challenging: because these patterns are culturally embedded and outside conscious control, users cannot easily avoid them through strategic self-presentation, and mechanistic analysis reveals that linguistic features are encoded in early transformer layers and entangled with other features. Our work calls for upstream consideration of the influences of linguistic variation to mitigate disparate impacts of LLM-mediated workplace communication.

</details>


### [155] [RippleMem: From Isolated Retrieval to Associative Recollection for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13334)
*Jingbo Ji,Lingyi Li,Xilong Cheng,Yuhao Zhou,Wenji Zhang,Yuting Tan,Yunxiao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RippleMem是一种基于自适应联想回忆的长时记忆系统，通过将交互历史存储为线索丰富的 episodic 记忆单元并组织成事件中心记忆图，替代一次性检索，实现多轮联想扩展以恢复缺失的证据，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上取得最佳性能，同时将图构建成本降低约30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体依赖外部记忆支持长时推理，但核心瓶颈不在于存储过往经验，而在于当相关信息分散在多次交互中时如何恢复正确的证据集。现有方法存在明显缺陷：全上下文方法需嘈杂的长上下文搜索，平面检索常返回孤立不完整的记录，基于图的记忆系统构建成本高且会压缩丰富的事件上下文。

Method: RippleMem包含三个关键设计：1) 将交互历史存储为线索丰富的episodic记忆单元；2) 组织成事件中心的记忆图结构；3) 采用两阶段检索机制：先通过混合线索回忆相关的记忆锚点，再从锚点沿语义和结构关联进行扩展，以恢复缺失的支持证据。初始回忆不仅作为答案上下文，还作为完成证据收集的线索。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，RippleMem在评估设置中取得最佳整体性能：在LoCoMo上LLM-as-a-Judge准确率提升3.95%，在LongMemEval-S上提升高达11.87%，同时图构建成本降低约30倍。

Conclusion: RippleMem验证了联想式回忆（而非一次性检索）对长时记忆系统的重要性，通过将已回忆的记忆作为线索来补全缺失证据，实现了更高效且更准确的记忆恢复，为解决LLM智能体的长期记忆访问问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon reasoning and interaction. However, the main bottleneck is not simply storing past experience, but recovering the right set of evidence when relevant information is distributed across many interactions. Existing approaches struggle with this access problem. Full-context methods require noisy long-context search, flat retrieval often returns isolated and incomplete records, and graph-based memory systems can be expensive to construct while compressing rich event context. We introduce RippleMem, a long-term memory system that replaces one-shot retrieval with adaptive associative recollection. Inspired by cue-dependent episodic retrieval and associative completion, RippleMem stores interaction history as cue-rich episodic memory units and organizes them in an event-centric memory graph. Given a query, it first recalls relevant memory anchors through hybrid cues, then expands from these anchors along semantic and structural associations to recover missing supporting evidence. In this way, initially recalled memories serve not only as answer context, but also as cues for completing the evidence needed to answer. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that RippleMem achieves the best overall performance across evaluated settings, improving LLM-as-a-Judge accuracy by 3.95% on LoCoMo and up to 11.87% on LongMemEval-S, while reducing graph construction cost by about 30x.

</details>


### [156] [CROP: Task Relevance via Counterfactuals for Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13387)
*Enhan Li,Junhao He,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CROP（Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation），一种用于选择性在线蒸馏（OPD）的令牌级监督分配方法。CROP通过释义校准的反事实敏感性裕度来量化任务相关性，为每个源提示构建原创-释义-反事实三元组，固定学生轨迹后测量每个响应位置对任务相关条件变化的敏感性，并以此为依据分配监督权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线蒸馏对所有响应令牌赋予等权监督，但不同令牌的监督价值不同。现有的选择性OPD标准主要关注优化需求（如不确定性或师生分歧），而任务相关性——监督是否与当前输入的语义内容相关——作为补充维度尚未被直接刻画。

Method: CROP通过释义校准的反事实敏感性裕度来操作化任务相关性。对每个源提示，构建经验证的原创-释义-反事实三元组，保持学生采样轨迹固定，测量每个响应位置对任务相关条件变化的敏感性，并用对保持语义改写（释义）的敏感性进行校准。

Result: 匹配选择对照实验表明，CROP比随机或最低相关性选择能识别出更有用的监督位置；组件对比证明了反事实敏感性和释义校准两者的价值。在两个师生设置中，CROP相对于最强的非CROP选择器分别提升了1.92和2.96个聚合性能点。

Conclusion: 任务相关性可作为选择性OPD的互补选择标准，CROP作为一种模型内部、对比特异性的令牌级监督分配方法具有有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student language model on trajectories sampled from its current policy, but assigns equal credit to response tokens with unequal supervision value. Selective OPD addresses this limitation by allocating supervision non-uniformly across response tokens according to their estimated training value. Most existing criteria, however, focus primarily on optimization need, such as uncertainty or teacher-student disagreement, while task relevance, namely whether the supervision is tied to the semantic content of the current input, remains less directly characterized as a complementary dimension. To address this gap, we introduce Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation (CROP), which operationalizes task relevance through a paraphrase-calibrated counterfactual sensitivity margin. For each source prompt, CROP constructs a validated original-paraphrase-counterfactual triplet, holds the student rollout fixed, and measures each response position by its sensitivity to a task-relevant condition change calibrated by its sensitivity to a meaning-preserving rewrite. Matched selection controls show that CROP identifies more useful supervision positions than random or lowest-relevance selection, while component comparisons confirm the value of both counterfactual sensitivity and paraphrase calibration. Across two teacher-student settings, CROP improves aggregate performance by 1.92 and 2.96 points over the strongest non-CROP selector. These results support task relevance as a complementary criterion for selective OPD and establish CROP as a model-internal, contrast-specific method for allocating token-level supervision.

</details>


### [157] [Motor, Cognitive, or Corpus? What Survives Cross-Lingual Transfer in Speech-Based Parkinsons Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13425)
*Serli Kopar,Sam Gijsen,Abner Hernandez,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对九个自监督学习（SSL）语音骨干网络进行了逐层分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针在三种语言上评估帕金森病（PD）检测。研究发现：最优表示层主要由源数据集而非SSL架构决定；迁移的判别信号缺乏病理特异性，PD分类器对痴呆语音也赋予高概率。这些发现揭示了语音病理识别模型在临床部署前需解决的重大局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音表示在单一语料库中能有效检测帕金森病，但不清楚模型捕获的是疾病相关特征还是数据集特定混杂因素。多数SSL骨干仅用健康语音预训练，其跨语料库泛化能力存疑，需要系统验证这些表示是否真正具备病理特异性。

Method: 对九个SSL语音骨干进行逐层分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针，跨三种语言进行评估。实验设计为多个场景，逐步引入参与者和记录的分布偏移，包括身份、录音条件、语言和病理类型等维度。

Result: 两个关键发现：(1) 层选择高度依赖于语料库——最佳表示层主要由源数据集决定，而非SSL架构本身；(2) 迁移的判别信号缺乏病理特异性——训练用于检测PD的分类器对目标语料库中的PD和痴呆语音赋予相似的高概率。

Conclusion: 当前SSL语音表示在病理识别方面的泛化能力存在根本性局限。模型可能过度依赖数据集特定特征而非通用病理模式。在语音病理识别模型可靠部署到临床环境之前，必须解决这些局限性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) speech representations achieve strong performance for Parkinson's disease (PD) detection within individual corpora. However, it remains unclear whether these models capture disease-related characteristics or exploit dataset-specific confounds, particularly since most SSL backbones are pretrained exclusively on healthy speech. To investigate this question, we perform a layer-wise analysis of nine SSL speech backbones using a low-capacity logistic regression probe across three languages. We structure the evaluation as multiple scenarios that progressively introduce distribution shifts in participant identity, recording conditions, language, and pathology. Our results reveal two key findings. First, layer selection is highly corpus-dependent: the optimal representation layer is determined primarily by the source dataset rather than by the SSL architecture itself. Second, the transferred discriminative signal lacks pathological specificity: classifiers trained to detect PD assign similarly high probabilities to both PD and dementia speech in the target corpus. These results highlight critical limitations that must be addressed before speech-based pathology recognition models can be reliably deployed in clinical settings.

</details>


### [158] [Are You Sure You're Sure? On the Impact of Instruction Tuning on Confidence and Lexical Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13430)
*Irina Proskurina,Mayank Kumar,Oyindolapo O. Komolafe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究指令调优对语言模型置信度与生成理由词汇多样性之间关系的影响。通过三组匹配的基座模型和指令调优模型在问答基准上的评估，发现指令调优一致性地改变模型置信度，同时跨理由多样性持续下降，而表面词汇多样性的变化方向和幅度因模型和基准而异，且这些差异在控制答案选择和理由长度后依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令调优的语言模型虽然在生成任务上表现优异，但最近被发现表现出过度的口头化自信（verbalized overconfidence）。在问答任务中，模型的口头化过度自信可能与生成支持性理由的一致性有关。作者希望探究指令调优引起的模型置信度变化是否伴随着生成答案理由词汇多样性的相应变化，从而理解置信度与理由多样性之间的关联。

Method: 作者选取三组匹配的基座模型与指令调优模型，在问答基准上评估。通过比较：(1)指令调优前后模型置信度的变化；(2)生成理由的跨理由多样性（cross-rationale diversity）和表面词汇多样性（surface-level lexical diversity）的变化；(3)在控制答案选择和理由长度后，检验置信度与理由多样性差异是否依然存在。

Result: 研究发现：(1)指令调优一致性地改变模型的答案置信度，尽管预测准确率变化有限且似然校准有所下降；(2)指令调优对理由多样性产生非均匀影响——跨理由多样性持续下降，而表面词汇多样性在不同模型和基准上的变化方向和幅度均不一致；(3)这些差异在控制答案选择和理由长度后依然存在。

Conclusion: 置信度和理由多样性捕捉了指令调优的不同效应，两者是相互独立的影响维度。指令调优虽然提升了模型某种程度的一致性（降低跨理由多样性），但并未改善似然校准，且置信度的改变与理由词汇多样性的变化并不呈现统一的对应关系。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models achieve strong performance across a range of generation tasks, but have also recently been shown to exhibit verbalized overconfidence. In question answering, verbalized model overconfidence may be associated with the consistency of the generated supporting rationales. In this paper, we study whether corresponding changes in the lexical diversity of generated answer rationales accompany changes in model confidence induced by instruction tuning. We evaluate three matched base and instruction-tuned models across question-answering benchmarks and find that instruction tuning consistently alters answer confidence, despite limited changes in predictive accuracy and decreases in likelihood-based calibration. Secondly, we observe a non-uniform effect of instruction tuning on rationale diversity: cross-rationale diversity consistently decreases, whereas surface-level lexical diversity varies in both direction and magnitude across models and benchmarks. Finally, we find that these differences persist after controlling for answer selection and rationale length, confirming that confidence and rationale diversity capture distinct effects of instruction tuning.

</details>


### [159] [Toward a Gricean Retreat: Probing LLMs for Knowledge Boundaries and Referent Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13484)
*Dananjay Srinivas,Saksham Khatwani,Maria Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过Gricean合作原则的视角，研究LLM在知识边界外编造细节而非退回到更安全笼统表述的问题。研究发现模型激活中确实编码了知识边界感和指称特异性信号，但这两个信号在生成时未被协调一致，导致模型即使面对未知实体也偏好具体指称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在面对知识边界外的实体时，倾向于编造貌似合理的细节而非退回到安全笼统的表述。作者用Gricean合作原则（明智的合作者会在不确定性时沿特异性层级后退，用信息量换取真实性）来框架这一失败，并探究LLM是否具备执行这种后退的必要成分。

Method: 使用基于T-REx构建的基准测试，该基准变化实体熟悉度和指称特异性两个维度。通过探测实验回答两个问题：(i)模型激活是否编码指称是否在知识边界内；(ii)模型是否能预知即将生成的指称的特异性程度。

Result: 两个问题的答案都是肯定的——模型激活中确实存在知识边界信号和特异性预测信号，但这两个信号在生成时没有被协调一致。模型即使在实体未知时也强烈偏好具体指称，甚至在提供正确的笼统替代选项时依然如此。

Conclusion: Gricean后退所需的底层原材料（知识边界感知和特异性预测）已经存在于模型中，但缺乏据此行动的策略。作者将此定位为迈向Gricean对齐的第一步——通过训练或引导目标将知识边界意识与生成时的指称特异性耦合起来。

Abstract: When asked about entities outside their knowledge boundary, LLMs routinely fabricate plausible-sounding details rather than backing off to safer, more general claims. We frame this failure through a Gricean lens: a cooperative speaker who is uncertain about a referent retreats up the specificity hierarchy, trading informativeness for truthfulness. We ask whether LLMs have the ingredients to perform this retreat. Using a T-REx-based benchmark that varies entity familiarity and referent specificity, we probe models to answer two questions: (i) do their activations encode whether a referent falls inside the knowledge boundary, and (ii) do they anticipate the specificity of the referent they are about to generate? We find that the answer to both is yes, but the two signals are not reconciled in generation. Models overwhelmingly prefer specific referents even when the entity is unknown to them, and do so even when offered correct generic alternatives. The substrate for a Gricean retreat is present, but the policy that would act on it is not. We position our findings as a first step toward Gricean alignment, training or steering objectives that couple knowledge-boundary awareness to referent-specificity during generation.

</details>


### [160] [Measuring Task-Agnostic Training Data Influence Across Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13515)
*Yuto Nishida,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yusuke Oda,Takashi Kodama,Chaoran Liu,Daisuke Kawahara,Yusuke Miyao,Max Müller-Eberstein,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的训练数据影响力度量方法，无需选择下游任务或验证集作为归因目标。该方法通过梯度更新对最终参数距离的缩减程度来定义示例影响力，可从中间检查点估计而不需重训练。应用于Pythia和PolyPythia的18种配置，发现训练早期文学类数据与最终参数轨迹更对齐，后期STEM数据更对齐，且该定性交叉在不同模型配置中一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型预训练中，持续一致地衡量训练数据影响力具有挑战性。现有方法依赖选择具有代表性的下游任务或验证集，且中间检查点的任务性能比较会因训练阶段不同而产生不一致，缺乏不依赖于特定任务选择的轨迹级影响力度量方法。

Method: 定义一个示例的影响力为其梯度更新减少到最终参数平方距离的程度，从中间检查点估计该量而无需重新训练。将该方法应用于Pythia和PolyPythia套件的18种配置，分析训练过程中有影响力数据的时序变化。

Result: 发现系统性时序变化：训练早期文学相关数据与最终参数轨迹更对齐，而STEM数据在后期变得更强对齐。这种定性交叉在多种模型配置中保持一致性。

Conclusion: 提供了一种可计算的轨迹级数据影响力视图，补充了以特定下游任务或验证集为定义的影响力分析，揭示了预训练过程中有影响力数据的动态转变模式。

Abstract: Measuring training data influence consistently across language model pretraining is challenging. It is difficult to select downstream tasks or validation sets representative of a model's general capabilities, and reliance on task performance at intermediate checkpoints complicates comparisons across training. We propose a measure of training data influence that does not require selecting a downstream task or validation set as the attribution target. Specifically, we define an example's influence by how much its gradient update reduces the squared distance to the final parameters of a given pretraining run, and estimate this quantity from intermediate checkpoints without retraining. Applying the method to 18 configurations from the Pythia and PolyPythia suites, we find systematic temporal changes in influential data. Early in training, literature-related data are more strongly aligned with the trajectory toward the final parameters, whereas STEM data become more strongly aligned in later stages. This qualitative crossover is broadly consistent across model configurations. Our results provide a tractable trajectory-level view of how influential data change throughout pretraining, complementing influence analyses defined with respect to specific downstream tasks or validation sets.

</details>


### [161] [DFM Mimir v1: An Open HRM Delivering Frontier Performance at 1B Parameters Using Only Permissible Post-Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13517)
*Peter Schneider-Kamp,Jacob Nielsen,Gianluca Barmina,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mimir v1，一个10亿参数的语言模型，基于Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM)架构从零训练，仅使用许可数据即可在英语上达到强竞争力表现，并在丹麦语上创下新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型开发依赖海量且往往未经许可的数据集，这为致力于开源和伦理数据来源的研究者设置了很高的门槛。作者希望通过仅使用许可数据来降低这一壁垒。

Method: 基于HRM架构训练10亿参数的模型，使用161个数据集的混合数据进行训练，从零开始训练模型。

Result: 在20个基准测试（涵盖英语、数学与代码、丹麦语）中，Mimir v1优于原始的HRM-Text 1B，并与更大的前沿模型如Qwen 3.5 4B和Gemma 4 E2B竞争。

Conclusion: 证明仅使用许可后训练数据也能训练出具有竞争力的语言模型，为开源和伦理数据研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Current large language model development relies on massive, often non-permissible datasets, creating a high barrier for researchers committed to open-source and ethically sourced data. We introduce Mimir v1, a 1-billion-parameter language model based on the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) architecture, that is trained from scratch and delivers highly competitive performance for English and sets a new state of the art for Danish using only permissible post-training data. Trained on a mixture of 161 datasets, Mimir v1 outperforms the original HRM-Text 1B and competes with larger frontier models like Qwen 3.5 4B and Gemma 4 E2B, tested across 20 benchmarks for English, Math & Code and Danish. The model is available on the Hugging Face Hub: https://huggingface.co/danish-foundation-models/DFM-Mimir

</details>


### [162] [SAEVerbalizer: Generating Explanations for Sparse Autoencoder Features via Representation Verbalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13538)
*Weihan Meng,Hongzhu Guo,Yi Jing,Dewen Liu,Zijun Yao,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAEVerbalizer是一个将稀疏自编码器（SAE）解码器方向注入LLM表示并微调下游层以生成自然语言解释的框架，解决了传统SAE特征解释依赖外部观察导致解释肤浅和计算效率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAE特征解释主要依赖外部观察模型行为，这导致两个问题：(1) 解释通常流于表面，仅基于观察到的行为推断；(2) 在大规模上收集行为证据的计算成本高昂。需要一种直接从SAE解码器方向生成解释的方法来克服这些限制。

Method: SAEVerbalizer框架将SAE解码器方向注入到LLM的表示中，然后微调LLM的下游层，使其能够为注入的特征生成自然语言解释。训练完成后，该"verbalizer"可以直接从解码器方向解释SAE特征。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 学到的语言化能力可以泛化到未见过的特征；(2) 可以在独立训练的SAE字典之间迁移；(3) 通过轻量级适配器可扩展到不同LLM的SAE特征。干预实验还显示，注入多个方向时解释会组合其含义，反转单个方向则产生相应的含义变化。

Conclusion: SAEVerbalizer成功实现了直接从SAE解码器方向生成特征解释，避免了外部行为观察的依赖，同时具有良好的泛化性、跨字典可迁移性和跨模型扩展性，为SAE特征解释提供了更高效且更深层的解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are proposed to extract numerous features from large language model (LLM) representations, yet explaining these features still relies primarily on external observation. This reliance leads to superficial explanations inferred from observed model behavior and computational inefficiency from collecting such behavioral evidence at scale. We introduce SAEVerbalizer, a framework that injects SAE decoder directions into an LLM's representations and fine-tunes the LLM's downstream layers to generate natural-language explanations of the injected features. Once trained, the resulting verbalizer explains SAE features directly from decoder directions, addressing both limitations. Our experiments show that the learned verbalization capability generalizes to unseen features, transfers across separately trained SAE dictionaries, and, with a lightweight adapter, extends to SAE features from different LLMs. Intervention experiments show that injecting multiple directions yields an explanation combining their meanings, while reversing individual directions produces corresponding meaning shifts.

</details>


### [163] [LittleLearner: Language Models Under Pedagogically Controlled Knowledge Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13545)
*Fanfei Li,Jana Zeller,Manuel Prada-Corral,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Prasanna Mayilvahanan,Ryan Cotterell,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LITTLECURRICULUM，一个精心策划的880亿token预训练语料库，仅限于美国小学五年级以下教学内容。基于此训练的50亿参数模型LITTLELEARNER具备足够的语言能力用于开放式评估，但知识和能力边界清晰可映射到可解释的课程指南，为研究模型知识获取提供了受控沙盒环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型在异构的海量网络文本上训练，导致研究知识习得和技能获取非常困难，因为无法准确刻画模型先前接触过的相关内容。作者希望创建一个受控环境，使训练数据的范围和边界明确可知，从而能够细致研究模型如何获取、表征和使用知识。

Method: 构建了LITTLECURRICULUM语料库（880亿token），严格限定为美国小学五年级以下的教学内容，排除超出该年级的概念、事实和词汇。从零开始训练50亿参数的LITTLELEARNER模型，并设计了一系列实验来测试通过后训练（post-training）和上下文学习（in-context learning）注入新知识的效果。

Result: LITTLELEARNER展现出足够的语言能力进行开放式评估，但其知识和能力边界清晰，与课程指南映射良好。后训练和上下文学习方法能够让模型更好地利用已有知识，但不会提升超出训练范围的能力。

Conclusion: 该受控沙盒环境对于研究模型知识获取、表征和使用机制具有重要价值，为未来关于模型能力边界、知识注入效果等方面的研究提供了可行的实验平台。

Abstract: Modern language models are trained on heterogeneous web-scale text corpora. Consequently, studying knowledge and skill acquisition is difficult, as prior exposure to related content is hard to characterize. To address this challenge, we introduce LITTLECURRICULUM, a curated 88B-token pretraining corpus tailored to U.S. elementary school material, explicitly excluding concepts, facts, and vocabulary taught above Grade 5. Training a 5B-parameter LLM from scratch on LITTLECURRICULUM yields LITTLELEARNER, a model with sufficient language competence for open-ended evaluation, yet with clear knowledge and capability boundaries mapped to interpretable curriculum guidelines. We release LITTLECURRICULUM and LITTLELEARNER as a developmentally restricted sandbox to study how models acquire, represent, and use data under a well-defined training scope. We illustrate the sandbox's utility in a first suite of experiments on injecting new knowledge through post-training and in-context learning. These methods let LITTLELEARNER better utilize existing knowledge, but do not raise out-of-scope capabilities. Our findings underscore the value of this controlled environment for future investigations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoKiFormer是一种新型大语言模型架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别增强序列内局部信息和序列外全局知识的建模，预训练收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练中存在两个低效问题：(i)自注意力缺乏局部性的归纳偏置，导致序列内部局部信息被冗余建模；(ii)混合专家（MoE）架构将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: LoKiFormer在标准decoder上增加两个专用模块：1) 局部融合注意力（LFA），在注意力中引入卷积融合，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力能够处理更具信息量的表示；2) 知识记忆模块（KMM），引入参数化的键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，实现存储与计算的解耦，支持直接的知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练中的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，验证了其在效率和效果上的优势。

Conclusion: 通过LFA和KMM两个模块的协同作用，LoKiFormer实现了序列内局部信息和序列外全局知识两个层面的更高效、更有效的信息整合，优于现有LLM架构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [165] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VGG-MADiffRL算法和MDCA分层控制架构，用于多AUV自组网协同目标跟踪，通过值梯度引导扩散策略生成动作，解决高维状态空间和噪声敏感问题，实现更快的收敛速度和更高的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网协同跟踪面临声学通信受限、拓扑动态变化和海洋扰动不确定等挑战。现有MARL方法存在高维联合状态-动作建模困难和策略对噪声敏感的问题，导致训练不稳定、跟踪性能退化。

Method: 提出MDCA三层闭环控制架构（全局智能控制层、局部在线训练层、物理动作执行层），实现任务分配、局部决策与执行反馈的协同优化。核心算法VGG-MADiffRL基于扩散策略，在逆向去噪过程中引入值梯度引导动作生成，使动作朝更高期望回报方向优化；采用双值网络联合优化和软目标更新缓解过估计与训练振荡。

Result: 实验表明VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中持续实现更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和工程实用性。

Conclusion: VGG-MADiffRL通过值梯度引导扩散去噪过程，显著提升了多AUV协同跟踪的稳定性与性能，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [166] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与卫星天气）提出并评估模型，预测尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区冰川湖溃决、降雨型滑坡及冰川池塘小洪水三类相关灾害的易感性（哪个地点）与触发时机（何时发生），并以严格的空间交叉验证对照强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期可行性研究已证明形变指示湖泊失稳、天气指示风险期，但未提出预测模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，仅用免费数据构建可预测"哪个地点易感"与"何时触发"的模型。

Method: 利用589起冰湖溃决事件（HMAGLOFDB）与数千起滑坡记录，将每起事件与相似但未失稳的站点匹配；采用空间交叉验证（按整幅地图瓦片留出）防止邻域记忆偏差。分别评估前置天气（时机）与地形（易感性），并对比五种深度学习模型与简单梯度提升基线及三规则决策树。

Result: 前置天气触发预测ROC：大溃决0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82；地形易感性在诚实匹配邻近站点后为0.76、0.71、0.54（小洪水不优于随机），但溃决信号在尼泊尔单区域可达0.89。深度学习未显著超过梯度提升基线，湖泊灾害中基线胜出。

Conclusion: 免费数据足以支撑灾害时机的时序预测，但地形易感性仅有部分可测能力，且深度学习无决定性优势。最终提供尼泊尔排名观察清单作为优先级辅助工具而非预测，并指出免费数据的应用边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [167] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History）架构，通过周期性缓存递归状态检查点作为状态锚点（state anchors），并为每个锚点生成内容条件化的锚键（anchor key），使状态空间模型能够在不牺牲计算效率的前提下，将记忆规模扩展到固定维度之外，从而在长上下文检索和推理任务中超越多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽具备强大的长上下文检索能力，但训练时二次方计算复杂度和推理时线性增长的KV缓存代价高昂。递归替代方案（如状态空间模型）虽然解码高效，但固定大小的状态压缩容易覆盖早期关联信息，导致在回忆密集型任务上表现不佳。现有方法难以同时兼顾长上下文记忆能力和计算效率。

Method: MARCH架构的核心设计包括：(1) 周期性地将累积的递归状态检查点缓存为状态锚点，使记忆库可随上下文长度增长；(2) 每个锚点关联一个紧凑的内容条件化锚键，实现基于内容的检索；(3) 每个token生成锚查询（anchor query），对所有因果可用的状态锚点进行注意力聚合；(4) 输出为沿当前状态对所有历史锚点的注意力式聚合，提供历史分辨率与记忆成本之间的可控权衡。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索等多个基准上一致超越多种线性注意力变体，证明内容路由的状态缓存能显著增强递归长程记忆能力，同时保持原生计算路径的效率。

Conclusion: 内容路由的状态缓存机制（MARCH）有效地弥合了Transformer的灵活长上下文记忆与递归模型的高效解码之间的差距，为状态空间模型提供了可扩展、可控制的记忆增长方案，同时不牺牲计算效率。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [168] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将生成建模统一表述为路径积分形式，其中基于流、扩散、变分和对抗的模型均是该主作用量下不同的评估原则。通过MSRJD形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，从而引入费曼图微扰论。该展开在无需随机采样成本的情况下为确定性采样器提供单圈修正，在可解和非线性漂移上得到验证（将树级误差从53%降至1.6%）。不完美学习的分数作为插入项，导出了响应加权分数匹配目标；对称等变漂移设计成为具有EFT幂次计数的算符展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（流模型、扩散模型、变分模型、对抗模型）缺乏统一的数学框架，且难以进行系统化的理论分析。本文旨在通过路径积分与MSRJD形式建立一个统一的主作用量框架，使不同生成模型范式可被统一理解，并为其引入量子场论中成熟的微扰论工具。

Method: 作者将生成建模表述为路径积分，利用MSRJD形式将自由项与相互作用项分离，从而开展费曼图微扰展开。该方法得到确定性采样器的单圈修正项（无需额外随机采样成本），并将不完美学习的分数视为插入项，同时将对称等变漂移设计转化为具有EFT（有效场论）幂次计数的算符展开。

Result: 在可解及非线性漂移场景中，单圈修正将树级误差从53%降至1.6%，验证了微扰论方法的有效性。此外，推导出了响应加权分数匹配目标，并建立了对称等变漂移设计的EFT幂次计数框架。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统一路径积分框架不仅连接了多种生成模型范式，还引入了一系列源自量子场论的分析工具（微扰论、算符展开、EFT幂次计数），为生成建模的理论分析和性能提升提供了新途径，尤其在不完美学习和对称性设计方面展现出应用潜力。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [169] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-AddGraph，首个为AddGraph（动态图边级异常检测的GCN+GRU框架）配备的事后可解释性框架。它通过双时空归因（DSTA）机制，在不改变检测性能（Delta AUC=0）的前提下，分别对空间结构、短期时序和长期时序三个维度提供归因解释，并在UCI Message基准上验证了其有效性和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图异常检测器（如AddGraph）虽然准确率高，但完全不透明——当边被标记为异常时，分析师只能得到一个分数而看不到任何理由。在需要审计和可信监管的协作信息系统中，这种不透明性是不可接受的，但AddGraph此前从未配备任何解释能力。

Method: 提出X-AddGraph事后可解释框架，核心是双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个与AddGraph架构模块对齐的组件：（1）基于梯度的空间相关性归因（针对当前邻接结构）；（2）直接读取推理时已计算好的上下文注意力权重（短期时序，零额外成本）；（3）通过循环隐状态进行梯度回滚（长期时序）。检测器保持冻结，解释完全事后生成。

Result: 检测性能完全保持（Delta AUC=0，经验验证到小数点后十位）。在UCI Message基准上，AddGraph基线平均快照AUC达0.8705，超过原论文结果；X-AddGraph逐分数复现且新增解释。在四类边群体评估中，长期归因识别历史快照的反事实信号显著优于随机选择（0.127 vs 0.074），这是空间盲解释器无法实现的能力。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph为AddGraph提供了首个严格事后、零性能损失的可解释性方案，填补了该领域可解释性的空白。长期时序归因能定位历史关键快照，为分析师提供有意义的反事实证据。代码已开源以供完全复现。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [170] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的睡眠阶段分期标注方法（LBH），利用多评分者数据构建更可靠的参考标注。通过混淆矩阵建模每个评分者的阶段特异性行为，聚合多个专家的概率后分配最终标签，在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上一致优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数自动睡眠分期研究仅与单一参考标注进行比较，忽略了评分者间的变异性。作者希望利用多评分者数据集，从多个专家的集体行为中构建更可靠的参考标签，从而提升睡眠分期模型的评估可靠性。

Method: 使用DOD-H和DOD-O数据集，提取EEG和chin EMG各30个特征（共60个）。LBH方法通过机器学习模型为每个评分者构建混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估计每个真实睡眠阶段的概率，再将所有评分者的概率聚合以分配最终标签。使用随机森林、SVM和多层感知机分类器，在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下评估。

Result: LBH在所有设置下一致优于数据集标注（DH）和最佳评分者标注（BSH）。最佳结果来自随机森林配合EEG+EMG：DOD-H上准确率86.07%、精确率85.46%、F1分数85.29%；DOD-O上准确率86.04%、精确率85.21%、F1分数84.70%。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模可以有效改进参考标注的构建，且不会丢弃来自单个专家的信息。该方法为处理睡眠分期中评分者间变异性的问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [171] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer模型中存在一个"预测方向"（预测token的unembedding方向）作为内容定义的privileged anchor。以该锚点为参照，残差流的变异性在几何和行为上都按与预测的接近程度分层。该分层在18个模型（7B-120B，稠密和MoE，基座和指令微调）中一致成立，并揭示了预测近端区域高度结构化、远端区域平坦且弱分类的新几何图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现Transformer会发展出privileged bases（坐标轴统计特性异于其他方向），但尚不清楚这种基座选择的是什么样的方向。作者希望理解预测方向（unembedding方向）是否以及如何作为内容定义的privileged anchor，从而解释高维计算如何与线性读出共存。

Method: 以预测方向的unembedding向量为锚点，测量残差流中各方向相对该锚点的几何与行为特性。对所有方差方向按与预测方向的接近程度分层，分析其结构、聚类判别能力、因果干预（扰动预测近端/远端方向观察模型行为变化）及模型规模扩展趋势。

Result: 预测方向在全部18个模型中均作为privileged anchor起作用。预测近端区域高度结构化、聚类相关prompts且因果上功能关键；远端区域平坦、anti-discriminative但在时间与因果上承担负载。预测方向几乎正交于主方差轴，导致基于方差的分析只能部分恢复该组织。行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Conclusion: 预测方向是与先前描述的坐标轴不同的privileged anchor。模型通过一个窄而scale-invariant的"预测接口"集中读出相关信息，同时利用广阔且随规模增长的远端空间承载计算，从而在高维空间中实现了线性读出与复杂计算的共存。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [172] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于机器学习的客观分类方法，用于对多种飞行状态下由随机阵风引起的压力载荷进行归类。通过将大量实验观测数据编码为机器学习表示，并应用总结程序选取最少但最具代表性的样本（exemplars），从而为所有观测提供基于相似性的客观分类标准。在包含3480个压力载荷测量、跨越六种飞行姿态的飞翼模型数据库上，该方法识别出了九个跨姿态重复出现的基本响应类型，对其瞬态响应的分析揭示了底层流体力学物理机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对阵风诱导载荷的分类通常依赖于粗略参数（如飞行姿态）进行标注，但这种分类标准缺乏客观性，且难以跨飞行条件组织复杂的载荷数据。作者希望找到一个客观的分类准则，既能组织不同飞行条件下的阵风载荷复杂性，又能保持基于粗参数标注的可解释性。

Method: 1) 用机器学习方法将大量实验观测编码为潜在表示；2) 应用总结（summarization）程序从表示中选取一个最小的、高度显著的样本子集作为基础样本（exemplars）；3) 以这些样本为判据，对所有观测进行基于相似性的客观分类。实验数据为3480个由随机阵风在飞翼模型上引起的压力载荷测量，覆盖六种飞行姿态。

Result: 该方法在3480个测量数据中发现了九个在不同飞行姿态下反复出现的基本响应类型。对这些类型的瞬态响应进行分析，能够获得对底层流体力学机制的物理直觉理解。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习表示和样本总结的方法能够提供客观且可解释的阵风载荷分类准则，识别出的基本响应类型可用于专家进一步检验和设计更精细的实验。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了临床预测模型中因治疗决策导致结局不可观测（即"偶然删失"）的问题。以心脏骤停后神经预后预测为案例（2497名患者，其中1429名结局不确定），提出将评估明确区分为确定病例与不确定病例的框架，并提出一个可利用两类标签进行权衡的简单预测模型。研究发现，具有相似确定病例AUROC的模型在Brier分数和不确定病例概率估计上可能存在显著差异，且提高不确定病例对齐度通常以牺牲确定病例准确性为代价，揭示了标准评估掩盖的重要权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型通常假设每个患者的结局都能被清晰观察到，但现实中治疗决策可能使临床相关的结局永久无法观测。标准评估指标（如AUROC）无法捕捉这类"不确定"患者的失败模式，而这些患者恰恰是最需要预后支持的群体。作者希望揭示并解决这一被忽视的问题。
